# Awesome Jev Cases

🌐 [简体中文](README.md) · **English**

An independent, source-linked catalog of Jev use cases and GitHub projects. Each case records the input, Jev decision, downstream action, and evidence limit. Project links lead to their original authors. This community repository is not affiliated with or endorsed by TypeSafe AI.

<!-- catalog:summary:start -->
**289 worked cases · 313 project listings · 27 official cookbooks**
<!-- catalog:summary:end -->

[Case library on the website](https://typesafe-jev.com/en/use-cases/) · [Project page](https://typesafe-jev.com/en/projects/) · [Official cookbooks](https://docs.typesafe.ai/cookbooks.md) · [Detailed catalog (Chinese)](CATALOG.md)

## Use cases

The table summarizes uses published by their builders. **Public code** means the implementation can be inspected; **author post only** means no inspectable code is linked. Neither means this site reproduced the API results, performance, or production outcome. Read each row's evidence limit; the [detailed catalog](CATALOG.md) is in Chinese.

<!-- catalog:cases:start -->
| Case | Scenario & input | Jev decision → action | Evidence & limit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [jev-ultrafast](https://github.com/browser-use/jev-ultrafast) | **Browser agent**<br>Visible page controls and the user's goal. | **Decision:** Jev selects an action and an indexed page element in one request; a small text model is used only for typing.<br>**Action:** The executor rechecks the target, acts in the browser, then independently verifies the outcome. The flight example stops at results; it does not book. | Code + author post<br>The repository's timing covers its own sample task, not general reliability.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/browser-use/jev-ultrafast) · [X](https://x.com/gregpr07/status/2100411066966749359) |
| [fast-jev-compaction](https://github.com/tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction) | **Coding agent**<br>Tool calls, results and surrounding context from an agent transcript. | **Decision:** Two Noul questions per eligible call ask whether the call and its full result still matter.<br>**Action:** Code thresholds keep, truncate or remove call/result pairs while user and assistant text stays intact. | Code + author post<br>Compaction can discard evidence needed later; on failure the library throws and leaves fallback to the caller.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction) · [X](https://x.com/tamarajtran/status/2100694549362553153) |
| [DocJev](https://github.com/jerryjliu/docjev) | **Document processing**<br>Page text from PDF, DOCX or PPTX files plus natural-language category rules. | **Decision:** Jev predicts document categories and boundaries within a combined packet.<br>**Action:** The program outputs categories, page ranges and review flags, and can export split PDFs. | Public code<br>Text extraction may run locally, but Jev inference is hosted; difficult OCR is a separate optional step.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/jerryjliu/docjev) |
| [pg-jev](https://github.com/realZachi/pg-jev) | **Database retrieval**<br>PostgreSQL rows and a natural-language condition written in SQL. | **Decision:** The extension batches one Noul question per row and also exposes Choice and Score functions.<br>**Action:** Probabilities return to SQL for filtering, sorting and grouping, with session-level caching by row content. | Code + author post<br>Evaluated row data can leave for an external API; latency and cost figures are measurements from the author's environment.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/realZachi/pg-jev) · [X](https://x.com/iam_zachi/status/2100679300756435135) |
| [hono-jev-router](https://github.com/yusukebe/hono-jev-router) | **Request routing**<br>An HTTP request's method, URL, redacted headers and part of its text body. | **Decision:** Each semantic route becomes a Noul question; all descriptions are evaluated in one Jev call.<br>**Action:** Code picks the first registered route above its threshold and falls through when none match. | Public code<br>The author calls this experimental and warns against using semantic routes for authentication or authorization.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/yusukebe/hono-jev-router) |
| [jev-guard](https://github.com/leepokai/jev-guard) | **Agent tool review**<br>A proposed coding-agent tool call, recent user instructions and content the agent read. | **Decision:** A Score rates risk; Noul questions examine approval need, user intent and instructions planted in untrusted content.<br>**Action:** Project code maps answers to deny, ask or allow and connects through host-specific hooks. | Public code<br>This community judgment layer does not replace host permissions, human approval or security boundaries.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/leepokai/jev-guard) |
| [typesafe-jev-examples · ticket triage](https://github.com/rajivkuriakose/typesafe-jev-examples) | **Support triage**<br>One support ticket. | **Decision:** One request asks Choice for the department, Score for business impact and Noul for churn signals.<br>**Action:** Code applies different routing thresholds by risk and keeps an other option for out-of-scope tickets. | Public code<br>This is a runnable example; the author reports testing the OpenRouter path, not the direct TypeSafe path.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/rajivkuriakose/typesafe-jev-examples) |
| [500-email classification demo](https://x.com/rileybrown/status/2100404532119269426) | **Email classification**<br>The 500 emails in the author's demo. | **Decision:** The author classifies the batch with Jev; the post does not disclose question types, labels or thresholds.<br>**Action:** The demo shows classifications; the author reports seconds of runtime and a 3.5-cent cost. | Author post only<br>Only the original post was checked; code, email samples and independent billing records were unavailable.<br>[X](https://x.com/rileybrown/status/2100404532119269426) |
| [Downloads folder sorter](https://x.com/marcelpociot/status/2100906882365788167) | **Personal file organization**<br>New files in a macOS Downloads folder and user-defined rules. | **Decision:** The author's example asks whether a file is an invoice; the post does not publish question types or fields.<br>**Action:** The app moves and renames files according to rules; the author says it uses no other language model. | Author post only<br>This is an author demo; source code, file extraction and error handling were not verifiable from the post.<br>[X](https://x.com/marcelpociot/status/2100906882365788167) |
| [3,282-post analysis](https://x.com/iannuttall/status/2100668908227162567) | **Content analysis**<br>The author's 3,282 X posts and their performance data. | **Decision:** Eight questions per post cover topic, hook, tone and whether it teaches something; the post does not state Jev question types.<br>**Action:** The author combines judgments with existing engagement data to identify patterns in better-performing posts. | Author post only<br>The sample is one account; cost and growth findings are self-reported and do not establish causality or cross-account performance.<br>[X](https://x.com/iannuttall/status/2100668908227162567) |
| [hn-oracle](https://github.com/anthony-maio/hn-oracle) | **Historical prediction analysis**<br>Historical Hacker News comments and their context. | **Decision:** A first-stage Noul judges whether a comment predicts the future; after a threshold, Noul, Choice and Score questions assess checkability, direction, subject and horizon.<br>**Action:** Code selects predictions for later hindsight grading, records structured fields and publishes results from a preregistered 1,000-comment pilot; the full archive has not been processed. | Public code<br>Accuracy, cost and calibration figures are reported by the author; probabilities need a separate calibration step, and full-archive scale and runtime remain projections.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/anthony-maio/hn-oracle) |
| [undertone](https://github.com/Nuu-maan/undertone) | **Message tone preview**<br>A message drafted before sending. | **Decision:** One Jev request uses Choice, Noul and Score to judge overall tone, signals such as sarcasm or pressure, urgency and whether the message suits a manager.<br>**Action:** The interface shows a tone label, color and phrase tips; the user decides whether to send. | Code + author post<br>There is no independent accuracy measurement; without an API key or on request failure the app uses an offline keyword classifier, so the entire demo cannot be attributed to Jev.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/Nuu-maan/undertone) · [X](https://x.com/Numankhannnnn/status/2102779770220286158) |
| [JevSearch web reranking](https://x.com/kylejeong/status/2102561749404971460) | **Web search**<br>A user query, selection criteria and roughly 25 candidate pages returned by Browserbase search. | **Decision:** Jev scores candidate results against the selected relevance criteria.<br>**Action:** The program returns five higher-scoring results; the author says Jev sometimes selects pages outside the search engine's initial top five. | Author post only<br>The author post and TypeSafe's repost were checked, but no public code, sample queries or independent relevance evaluation was found. This is not a TypeSafe project.<br>[X](https://x.com/kylejeong/status/2102561749404971460) · [TypeSafe X](https://x.com/typesafeai/status/2103218258405118035) |
| [Jev Audit](https://github.com/neozhu/jev-audit) | **Contract text comparison**<br>A baseline contract and OCR text extracted from a scanned copy. | **Decision:** One Jev call uses Noul, Choice and Score to judge substantive term agreement, signs of edits, difference type and closeness of terms.<br>**Action:** Code combines answers into a weighted consistency score; the author's policy passes above 90%, while other results go to human review in an inspectable report. | Public code<br>Contract text goes to the TypeSafe API; optional question generation also calls OpenAI. This site did not run the API or validate legal conclusions. The 90% cutoff is the author's choice, and original terms still need human review.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/neozhu/jev-audit) |
| [OpenRouter model selection with Jev](https://x.com/OpenRouter/status/2103610898690855161) | **Model routing**<br>An incoming OpenRouter LLM request and conversation context; the author's post says routing is cache-aware. | **Decision:** Jev judges task type, difficulty, precision needs and likely benefit from a larger model, then selects a model and reasoning effort.<br>**Action:** OpenRouter forwards the request to the selected model and includes routing metadata; its post says a Jev timeout or invalid output fails the request. | Author post only<br>The OpenRouter post, product page and TypeSafe repost were checked, but no public router source or independent quality and cost evaluation was found. This is a third-party product, not a TypeSafe first-party project.<br>[OpenRouter X](https://x.com/OpenRouter/status/2103610898690855161) · [OpenRouter details](https://x.com/OpenRouter/status/2103610988432126195) · [TypeSafe X](https://x.com/typesafeai/status/2103612889655353346) · [OpenRouter](https://openrouter.ai/typesafe/jev-router) |
| [jevgrep code-context retrieval](https://github.com/dzhng/jevgrep) | **Coding-agent retrieval**<br>A coding-task question, repository hierarchy, file previews and source units. | **Decision:** Jev uses bounded yes-or-no relevance judgments to select folders, files and code locations across the repository hierarchy.<br>**Action:** The CLI returns verbatim source excerpts with line references and reading leads; the coding agent then edits and tests separately. | Public code<br>Eligible source content is sent to the chosen external Jev provider. The author's cost and solve-rate figures apply only to their sample, and retrieval may miss relevant files.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/dzhng/jevgrep) |
| [wellposed Jev request lint](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed) | **Request quality checks**<br>The state, question types, instructions and criteria in a proposed Jev request. | **Decision:** Offline rules catch structural defects; for semantic issues they cannot settle, the tool asks Jev Noul questions, such as whether a realistic input fits none of the offered choices.<br>**Action:** The program reports warnings and suggested fixes before a production request; the developer decides whether to revise it. | Public code<br>Semantic linting itself needs a TypeSafe API key and may send request content. The author's labeled samples and accuracy figures were not independently reproduced; warnings do not replace testing.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed) |
| [jevmod community moderation](https://github.com/ohernandezdev/jevmod) | **Community moderation**<br>A message's text, channel topic and community-defined rules. | **Decision:** Jev answers separate Noul questions for categories such as scams, spam and harassment, returning a probability for each.<br>**Action:** Policy thresholds flag messages for moderator review; deletion is off by default and requires explicit enablement. | Public code<br>Message text and channel topic go to TypeSafe; on Jev failure the program leaves messages alone and logs the error. The author's accuracy and cost figures were not independently reproduced, and human review remains necessary.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/ohernandezdev/jevmod) |
| [jev-mobile Android control loop](https://github.com/Friedjof/jev-mobile) | **Android device control**<br>A user goal, semantic Android UI state and the set of currently valid actions. | **Decision:** Jev uses Choice to select the next step from actions supplied by code; it does not generate arbitrary coordinates or code.<br>**Action:** The worker journals the mutation, acts on the device, observes again and uses separate checks to decide whether the task is complete or needs escalation. | Public code<br>The flow needs an authorized USB-debugging device and TypeSafe API; UI context may leave the device. This site did not reproduce it on a phone, and risky actions and completion depend on the project's own approval and verification logic.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/Friedjof/jev-mobile) |
| [TypeSafe computer-use assistant](https://github.com/awlevin/typesafe-computer-use) | **Computer use**<br>A user goal, screen OCR or accessibility state and actions allowed by the program. | **Decision:** Jev uses Choice to select an action kind, target control or site, and can use Noul to check whether typed text suits a field.<br>**Action:** The executor drives the mouse and keyboard deterministically, then captures the next screen state before continuing. | Public code<br>This beta controls a real desktop. Screen content goes to TypeSafe, and optional text generation uses another model. This site did not verify speed, cost or OSWorld results; users should start with a dry run.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/awlevin/typesafe-computer-use) |
| [foreman](https://github.com/thruwire/foreman) | **Coding-agent supervision**<br>A ticket, spec, or bug report plus factory evidence such as diffs and tests produced by Codex or OpenCode workers. | **Decision:** One Jev call returns parallel probabilities for implementation completeness, requirement satisfaction, test sufficiency, verification need, worker health, and human escalation.<br>**Action:** Code decides to continue, steer, stop, retry, verify, or finish based on thresholds; Jev only supervises and never writes code. | Public code<br>The repository states effectiveness depends on configured checks, evidence, thresholds, and models; this site read the code and docs without independently reproducing results.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/thruwire/foreman) |
| [minecraft-agent](https://github.com/rmalde/minecraft-agent) | **Game agent**<br>A planning goal plus live state from a vanilla Minecraft server; a read-only Java sensor can report the exact dragon-head position. | **Decision:** GPT-6 Astra plans while Jev selects each player action; the author's best verified run used 131 Jev decisions and 35 Astra calls.<br>**Action:** Selected actions are sent through the normal player protocol; game rules and entity state stay untouched. | Public code<br>The 8m43s run is the author's best recording and dragon timings vary per run; recordings stay local and were not replayed by this site.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/rmalde/minecraft-agent) |
| [typesafe-mario](https://github.com/fhshaik/typesafe-mario) | **Game agent**<br>Emulator telemetry and RAM parsed into compact JSON covering Mario's motion, jump arc, enemies, terrain, and past control results; no screenshots. | **Decision:** A Choice picks one of seven legal controller actions (noop, right, right_jump, …) and returns the full probability distribution.<br>**Action:** The emulator advances several frames and the loop repeats; latency, probabilities, and confidence are logged to local JSONL. | Public code<br>No ROM ships with the repo and a legal copy is required; the 8-frames-per-decision default is experimental, with no published completion benchmark.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/fhshaik/typesafe-mario) |
| [mobile-jev](https://github.com/droidrun/mobile-jev) | **Mobile agent**<br>Screen state from a Mobilerun Android phone plus a natural-language goal. | **Decision:** Jev selects each step from available actions; the Uber demo completes nine actions in about 21 seconds, from opening the app to payment selection.<br>**Action:** Actions run on a real phone through the Mobilerun API with execution traces and per-request latency; the sample task stops at payment selection. | Public code<br>Timing comes from the author's recorded demo; device and Mobilerun charges are extra, no booking is completed, and this site did not rerun it.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/droidrun/mobile-jev) |
| [jev-voice-browser](https://github.com/moritzkremb/jev-voice-browser) | **Voice browser control**<br>Partial speech transcripts streamed from the Web Speech API plus an indexed snapshot of up to 100 elements on the controlled page. | **Decision:** Each partial transcript fires one Jev request with 9-11 questions on intent, target element, site, completeness, addressee, and destructiveness, returning in ~250-350 ms.<br>**Action:** Code decides to act, wait, ask, or ignore; queries and URLs are extracted as candidate spans that Jev only picks verbatim — it never writes text. | Public code<br>The microphone relies on Chrome/Edge Web Speech; latency and the ~$0.0002-per-call figure are the author's measurements.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/moritzkremb/jev-voice-browser) |
| [jev-drone](https://github.com/RomanSlack/jev-drone) | **Robotics**<br>Depth and segmentation from the onboard camera, compressed by classical CV into five forward sectors and obstruction heights; Jev does no perception. | **Decision:** At 2.5 Hz one call asks three questions — a Choice of maneuver, a Score of risk, and a Noul on whether the target is truly lost; scene fingerprinting caches repeats (~110 calls per 65 s flight).<br>**Action:** Tactical judgment is advisory; a 50 Hz safety reflex and a 500 Hz geometric controller hold veto power, for example climb is refused unless the measured obstruction top is reachable. | Public code<br>A five-station course in MuJoCo simulation; figures come from the author's flight runs, not a physical drone, and were not replayed here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/RomanSlack/jev-drone) |
| [jev-codex-router](https://github.com/0xNatoshi/jev-codex-router) | **Model routing**<br>A bounded decision state from the Codex session; the full canonical replay goes only to the executing model, never to Jev. | **Decision:** For every model call, including continuations after tools, Jev picks the model and thinking effort together, targeting sufficient capability without wasting quota.<br>**Action:** Requests route through the embedded Codex Router fork to luna/terra/sol/astra; any Jev error fails open and traffic passes through unchanged. | Public code<br>The README's ~60% saving is a backtest of 237 turns under an old policy, explicitly not measured quota saved nor evidence for the current policy.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/0xNatoshi/jev-codex-router) |
| [jev-pruner](https://github.com/tamaratran/jev-pruner) | **Context compression**<br>Bash stdout right after Claude Code runs it (outputs under 10,000 estimated tokens pass through) plus the task and session history. | **Decision:** Output is chunked and each chunk gets one noul — does any line still need to be available? A single needed line protects the chunk, including values earlier instructions depend on.<br>**Action:** Unneeded chunks are dropped and the rest flows on untouched; errors, JSON/XML/diff output, whole-document commands like git diff, and recognized documents are exempt. | Public code<br>Independent of the author's fast-jev-compaction; token counts are estimates, not exact tokenizer output, and compression was not measured here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/tamaratran/jev-pruner) |
| [neo4jev](https://github.com/jexp/neo4jev) | **Graph retrieval**<br>The outgoing relationships of the current Neo4j node (type, properties, target label/properties), with schema discovered live by introspection. | **Decision:** A Choice picks the next relationship while a noul asks whether the goal is reached, riding in the same call so each hop costs one round trip.<br>**Action:** Probabilities feed a top-k beam search ranked by summed log-probability, and the path is rendered interactively with its neighborhood. | Public code<br>Notebooks and the Streamlit app run against the public companies2 graph; without a key, failures show verbatim and clearly labelled stand-in answers drive the pipeline — never presented as TypeSafe output.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/jexp/neo4jev) |
| [pi-jev](https://github.com/y0usaf/pi-jev) | **Agent tool review**<br>Pending bash/write/edit calls from the Pi coding agent, plus what a command printed after it ran. | **Decision:** The gate bundles three nouls (destructive, exfiltration, beyond scope) and a four-level impact score into one request; an output judge adds two questions on secrets and failure class.<br>**Action:** Shadow mode only notifies by default and can switch to pre-run confirmation; every error path fails open — a missing key, timeout, or 429 lets the tool call proceed. | Public code<br>Thresholds (0.90/0.70/0.85/2.50) are the author's defaults; this layer does not replace host permissions or real security boundaries.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/y0usaf/pi-jev) |
| [jevmeter](https://github.com/ChetasLua/jevmeter) | **Video content analysis**<br>Sentence-level transcript of any video plus a chosen preset (debates, earnings calls, podcasts, or pitches). | **Decision:** Each sentence is scored against the preset, flagging dodged questions, emotional appeals, self-contradictions, and hype; the author reports 99% held-out preset accuracy.<br>**Action:** Results render into a postable 16:9 edit; a full debate costs about $0.05 to score. | Code + author post<br>Accuracy and cost are the author's self-reported evals, not reproduced here; presets target mostly English audio and video.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/ChetasLua/jevmeter) · [X](https://x.com/chetaslua/status/2100473581251748216) |
| [jev-shell-history](https://github.com/mrnugget/jev-shell-history) | **Terminal productivity**<br>The half-typed command line and the last 100 distinct history entries. | **Decision:** A Choice ranks which history entry the input is most likely completing; a literal prefix match completes directly, otherwise a replace mode suggests the best fuzzy match.<br>**Action:** A fish-style grey suggestion with its score appears after the cursor; press → or ^E to accept, with limits and thresholds configurable. | Public code<br>Keystrokes can trigger API calls (gated by a 2-character minimum and thresholds) at the user's expense; the demo GIF uses fabricated history.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/mrnugget/jev-shell-history) |
| [jev-chat](https://github.com/w3cj/jev-chat) | **Tool calling**<br>The user's message, conversation state, and earlier tool results; only a TYPESAFE_API_KEY is required. | **Decision:** Choices pick which MCP tool to call, where each argument comes from, whether to confirm first, and the reply type; a noul checks whether fields like a due date are stated.<br>**Action:** Code calls the tools and builds replies from their own data — no model writes text, so every value on screen is either typed by the user or returned by a tool. | Public code<br>An inspector shows the full request, probabilities, and code actions for any reply; the bundled tool set is small and not a general chat replacement.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/w3cj/jev-chat) |
| [typesafe-adblock](https://github.com/realZachi/typesafe-adblock) | **Browser extension**<br>Ad-shaped DOM candidates pre-filtered by code rules, compressed into compact JSON with tag, classes, link hosts, and standard IAB sizes. | **Decision:** One request per batch carries a noul per candidate — is this a paid advertisement? — returning one probability per element.<br>**Action:** Elements with P(ad) ≥ 0.70 are outlined, shrunk, and removed; a MutationObserver catches lazy-loaded ads, capped at 30 candidates per batch. | Public code<br>The author calls it a fun project, not a real ad blocker: it costs tokens per page, misses ads, eats non-ads, and does nothing about tracking or video ads.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/realZachi/typesafe-adblock) |
| [jevmail](https://github.com/fazlerocks/jevmail) | **Email triage**<br>Gmail messages fetched with a single read-only gmail.readonly scope and only list/get-style methods. | **Decision:** Three questions per message: which of five trays (needs reply, updates, promos, sales, spam), a 1-5 urgency score, and whether a human wrote it to you.<br>**Action:** Mail sorts into trays showing top-two probabilities, with corrections stored beside original answers in local SQLite; 1,000 emails take about a minute and around 3 cents. | Public code<br>Read-only — it never sends, archives, or labels; speed and cost are the author's measurements through the Vercel AI Gateway.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/fazlerocks/jevmail) |
| [HA-Jev](https://github.com/AboveColin/HA-Jev) | **Smart home**<br>House and sensor state, plus Noul/Choice/Score questions defined by the user in the UI or YAML. | **Decision:** Every question becomes an entity — a probability, an option with its distribution, or a scale value — while four actions (jev.noul, jev.choice, jev.score, jev.ask) answer inside automations.<br>**Action:** Entities feed automations and Assist voice routing; the integration reports daily calls, input tokens, and estimated cost against a daily budget. | Public code<br>A HACS custom integration whose author states it is not affiliated with TypeSafe; judgment quality depends on question design and automation outcomes rest on user config.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/AboveColin/HA-Jev) |
| [shapeshift](https://github.com/anishfn/shapeshift) | **UI intent classification**<br>Live typing in a single text box, such as "dinner with priya friday 8pm on zoom" or "split 2400 between 3". | **Decision:** One Jev call answers 14 parallel questions: which card the input should morph into (event, checklist, timer, bill splitter, color picker, poll, …) plus signals like "is it a video call?" or "is it urgent?".<br>**Action:** Dates, amounts, units, and math are resolved by deterministic code; without a key or on API errors it silently falls back to a built-in keyword classifier and stays fully offline. | Public code<br>The online key is read only server-side; the offline fallback means the default experience does not reflect Jev accuracy, and no A/B comparison was made here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/anishfn/shapeshift) |
| [jevals](https://github.com/openlayer-ai/jevals) | **Agent evaluation**<br>An agent trace (OpenAI-format messages and tool schemas; Anthropic content blocks and LangChain messages also pass directly). | **Decision:** One request returns eight parallel judgments — tool choice, used tool result, groundedness, scope, relevancy, completeness, indirect injection, and PHI.<br>**Action:** One request per trace at a few thousandths of a cent in a few hundred milliseconds, cheap enough for every trace and the agent loop; backends include TypeSafe/Vercel, local Kev or Laya, or a plain LLM. | Public code<br>Example figures come from the author's quickstart trace; the validity of each metric was not independently evaluated here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/openlayer-ai/jevals) |
| [winnow](https://github.com/GhalebDweikat/winnow) | **Context compression**<br>Large Read, Bash, or Grep results in Claude Code, split into roughly 25-line blocks. | **Decision:** Each block gets one noul — is it needed for the current task? Only confident-no blocks are hidden; uncertain blocks and outputs that look like errors always stay.<br>**Action:** Hidden blocks become a three-line stub: what was hidden, a one-line summary from a cheap model, and a key that restores the full text on demand. | Public code<br>It leans on the judge's calibration, with an uncalibrated Claude Haiku adapter offered as a fallback; thresholds are configurable and savings were not measured here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/GhalebDweikat/winnow) |
| [jev-chat-jarvis](https://github.com/jev-chat/jev-chat-jarvis) | **Chat copilot**<br>The on-screen conversation captured on the user's own device — accessibility-tree reading on Android, window screenshots plus local OCR on Windows; only conversations the user can already see. | **Decision:** Jev judges the other side's intent and emotional tension and ranks candidates; a generative model drafts three replies, each shown with the win probability Jev assigned.<br>**Action:** A translucent overlay shows the judgment and candidates with one-click copy/fill into the input box; sending always stays manual and transfers or red packets are never touched. | Public code<br>Judgments run through OpenRouter or direct TypeSafe access with user-supplied keys; users are responsible for lawful screen reading, no eval data ships in the README, and this site did not test it.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/jev-chat/jev-chat-jarvis) · [GitHub · Windows 版](https://github.com/jev-chat/jev-chat-windows) |
| [Glean offline expert routing pilot](https://x.com/tonygentilcore/status/2104639390266036251) | **Enterprise model routing**<br>751 historical requests with golden expert-route labels, using a three-expert simplification of the production router. | **Decision:** Jev chooses one of three predefined experts for each request, replacing the bounded selection step of the generative routing prompt.<br>**Action:** The team compares choices with golden labels and its LLM router, then separately measures latency on 40 actual-transfer examples; the article does not claim deployment. | Author post only<br>This is an author-reported offline evaluation without public code or independent reproduction. The 8.1× median speedup comes from 40 transfer examples; data residency and operational guarantees remain open.<br>[X article](https://x.com/tonygentilcore/status/2104639390266036251) · [TypeSafe X](https://x.com/typesafeai/status/2104772007481180633) |
| [jevwright browser business-flow tests](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevwright) | **Browser testing**<br>Natural-language business steps, controls on the current Chromium page, and declared API requests and assertions. | **Decision:** Jev grounds each step to a control on the live page and makes bounded checks of visible state; it does not decide whether the test passed.<br>**Action:** Playwright acts and records a semantic path for later model-free replay; requests, API/database assertions and code monitors determine pass or fail. | Public code<br>Initial grounding or healing needs an OpenRouter/Vercel gateway key; replay does not. Code and CI are public, but costs and outcomes are author-reported; this site did not run a live-site test.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevwright) · [Source code](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevwright/blob/main/src/models.ts) |
| [IRS form-page classification with confidence gate](https://github.com/kyotofin/tax-doc-classifier) | **Tax-document routing**<br>Text extracted from one PDF page plus repository-generated IRS form and page-kind options; code handles blank pages directly. | **Decision:** Jev Choice selects a form and one of seven page kinds, with a second choice for a few form families, returning probabilities.<br>**Action:** Code emits a form ID, page kind and confidence; it gates downstream routing at 0.95 by default and leaves low-confidence handling to the caller. | Public code<br>Limited to English federal forms. Scans need OCR, while state forms are only detected by kind. Page text goes to TypeSafe; accuracy, speed and cost are author-reported and were not remeasured or used for tax filing here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/kyotofin/tax-doc-classifier) · [Source code](https://github.com/kyotofin/tax-doc-classifier/blob/main/src/backend.ts) |
| [NetHack legal-action selection](https://github.com/statico/jev-nethack) | **Game agent**<br>The current NetHack terminal view, player state, and legal actions enumerated by code for this turn. | **Decision:** Jev uses Choice to pick the next step only from the legal-action list, without generating arbitrary keystrokes.<br>**Action:** The project motor maps the selected action to keystrokes, then reads the resulting screen for the next turn. | Public code<br>The default run needs a local NetHack build and a paid TypeSafe API key; source also supports a compatible local server. The repository shows runs and decision logs, but this site did not verify ascension, win rate, or cost.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/statico/jev-nethack) · [Source code](https://github.com/statico/jev-nethack/blob/main/jev/jevapi.py) |
| [jevvium mobile acceptance-test generation](https://github.com/AndresCarreonDiaz/jevvium) | **Mobile testing**<br>A YAML acceptance goal, the current Appium screen and actionable controls, and optional explicit assertions. | **Decision:** In one request, Jev uses Choice to select the next action, Noul to judge goal completion, and closed choices for field values.<br>**Action:** Appium performs the action; once the goal and assertions hold, the tool writes a WebdriverIO test with fixed selectors and validates it through model-free Appium replay. | Public code<br>Requires Appium, a device or simulator, and a TypeSafe key. Without explicit expect assertions, a pass may rest only on the model judgment; author-reported timings and pass rates were not reproduced here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/AndresCarreonDiaz/jevvium) · [Source code](https://github.com/AndresCarreonDiaz/jevvium/blob/main/src/providers/jev.ts) |

Recall, injection screening, and cost figures in the README are author-reported and were not remeasured here.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/MersivMedia/jev-rag-retrieval) · [Source code](https://github.com/MersivMedia/jev-rag-retrieval/blob/main/jev_retrieval/jev/client.py) |

The 75-query evaluation is small; speed, cost, and accuracy are author-reported, not independently reproduced or proof of deployment.<br>[Author X](https://x.com/rox_ai/status/2104642687534293353) · [Method thread](https://x.com/rox_ai/status/2104642689455292721) · [TypeSafe X](https://x.com/typesafeai/status/2105356405998063911) |

Author smoke tests do not establish accounting accuracy or savings; this site did not submit real transactions, call the Gateway or reproduce accounting outcomes.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/darrentmorgan/jev-accounting) · [Source](https://github.com/darrentmorgan/jev-accounting/blob/b9548f98e2f172d6096c258c7c7a9a69ee83ba7c/src/classifier.ts) · [Source](https://github.com/darrentmorgan/jev-accounting/blob/b9548f98e2f172d6096c258c7c7a9a69ee83ba7c/src/catalog.ts) |

Optional Shingi is a separate model whose calibration cannot be assumed valid for this app.<br>[GitHub](https://github.com/kortexa-ai/mappity) · [Source](https://github.com/kortexa-ai/mappity/blob/ab91ebb7a24d125e70d858d613e31719e5306a46/server/jev.js) · [Source](https://github.com/kortexa-ai/mappity/blob/ab91ebb7a24d125e70d858d613e31719e5306a46/server/pipeline.js) |

Local storage does not mean local inference: selected evidence goes to hosted Jev, and recall is bounded by the ingested corpus.<br>[src/sets/jev.ts](https://github.com/Zilize/parallax-dsh/blob/023ffadd6dea3af30587b89feb6073ffdd142c68/src/sets/jev.ts) · [src/sets/jobs.ts](https://github.com/Zilize/parallax-dsh/blob/023ffadd6dea3af30587b89feb6073ffdd142c68/src/sets/jobs.ts) · [src/jev-defaults.ts](https://github.com/Zilize/parallax-dsh/blob/023ffadd6dea3af30587b89feb6073ffdd142c68/src/jev-defaults.ts) · [README.md](https://github.com/Zilize/parallax-dsh/blob/023ffadd6dea3af30587b89feb6073ffdd142c68/README.md) |

Off, late, failed or low-confidence model results fall back to deterministic priorities without blocking the event loop. | Public code<br>MIT community code. Native SDK wiring, live/offline-mode separation and the consumer were reviewed without cameras, game execution or model calls. <!-- catalog:cases:end -->

## GitHub projects

**Official** is reserved for first-party `typesafe-ai/*` repositories. **Inspired replicas** do not release Jev's model weights. Stars and verification dates are snapshots, not quality ratings; confirm licensing in the original repository. An `unknown` license does not grant open-source reuse rights.

## Data and updates

The tables are generated from [`data/cases.json`](data/cases.json) and [`data/projects.json`](data/projects.json). [`data/official-recipes.json`](data/official-recipes.json) tracks TypeSafe's official cookbooks; see the [detailed catalog](CATALOG.md) for their source links. Run `node scripts/validate.mjs` to check the data shape, then `node scripts/render.mjs` to refresh both README languages and the catalog. `node scripts/render.mjs --check` detects stale generated tables.

The editorial workflow is in [`docs/codex-daily-curation.md`](docs/codex-daily-curation.md): Codex searches GitHub, original X posts in Chrome, and official sources separately, then judges primary evidence without a hard star threshold. **The native Scheduled heartbeat `jev` is active at 12:17 Japan time daily**. Verified content from any successful source can be published while a failed source is marked separately and its backfill cursor is retained. All three X Latest searches returned real timelines on October 3, but date filters still returned out-of-range posts, so the historical backfill gap remains open.

## Contributing

Recommend a case in an Issue or PR with its original repository or author post, the actual call site, license, input, and downstream action. Do not submit API keys, user data, or large copies of third-party code. Report bugs in a linked project to its original maintainer.
